1 About 
This document describes the SIF APIs, which can be used by external systems to create, update and read 
data to/from 360°. The SIF APIs consist of SOAP, REST and RPC services. The SOAP and REST endpoints 
are for 360° on-premises installations, and the RPC endpoints are for 360° Online (cloud solutions) and 
on-prem installations above version 5.0.  
«SIF RPC was released in 2018 and is the preferred SIF interface to use when developing integrations 
for communicating with 360. SIF SOAP and SIF REST are based on old, outdated technology and will 
reach end of life Q1 2022. When end of life is reached, customers using SIF SOAP or SIF REST 
must migrate their client code to use SIF RPC before they can upgrade to the next version released 
of 360°.  
From 360° version 5.9, we have provided the support for old SIF SOAP based services with RPC way of 
calling called SOAP bridge by introducing a new endpoint “SIF SOAP” under web admin -> Endpoint 
configuration just like “SIF RPC”. This bridge component is implemented as a Asp.net REST service but will 
translate the SOAP message and call the existing implementation. This new SOAP bridge will be available 
only on limited 360° versions, going further we will discontinue the SOAP and REST services. 
 
See the Overview model for what is available in the different services.  
 
 A simple figure describing the different APIs and how SIF integrates with 360°. The SIF core library is also used for custom 
integrations. 
 
1.1 Installing the API 
Installation is done by Tieto and is usually done on the same server that 360° is installed on. 

--- PAGE 15 ---
 
SIF APIs - Documentation 
 
16 Feb 2026
Public
 
© Tieto

Page 15 of 395 
 
Mandatory pre-requisite for installing SIF from 1.3.0 release and onwards: 
Important: As SIF has upgraded to .Net framework 4.8, it is mandatory to have 4.8 framework and .Net 
Core 3.1 
With 360° version 5.9, SIF APIs (SIF RPC and SIF SOAP bridge) are part of standard 360° installation. 
1.2 Updating the API 
Updating is done by Tieto and is done by re-installing the service for 360° version 5.8 or below. 
We recommend that any client consuming the SOAP webservice is rebuilt and tested with the updated 
WSDL, if this is necessary or not depends on the consumer of the webservice, which we have no control 
over. The reason rebuilding may be needed is a rare scenario which can result in input DataMembers 
(properties) being nulled out. The scenario is that DataMembers can have been added to an input 
contract that change the DataMembers alphabetical order. If a webservice client does not generate and 
send the xml in the correct order, the wrongly ordered properties will be null. Read more about this on SO, 
MSDN-1 and MSDN-2. 
As an extra precaution, we have since January 2017 started ordering new input DataMembers so 
changes will be fully backwards compatible. However, previously added input DataMembers have not 
and cannot be ordered as the correct order cannot be determined without considering every consumer of 
the web services, which is impossible. 
For 360° version 5.9 or above, to get latest changes in API, installation of 360° standard setup or hotfix is 
required. (This should be done by Tieto) 
1.3 Versioning and continuous development 
If you have SIF SOAP or RPC newer than November 2018, you can check which version you have by using 
the GetSIFVersion operation in SupportService. 
On premise, you can check what version of the service you have by checking the file version of a dll. 
These are typically found under C:\360\360 Program Files\instances\[instance 
name]\webservices\SI.WS.Core\SIF\bin, right click on the SI.Data.Core.dll > Properties > Details > File 
version. This number is the build version of the web service installation and corresponds to our internal 
changeset ID.

If your file version is 1.0.0.0 it means it was built before mid-2015 when versioning was implemented. 
 
The API is continuously improved with changes that in almost all cases is backwards-compatible. 
Meaning an existing integration should not break on contract or behavior level if you chose to update the 
API. There are two exceptions: 
- 
SOAP: Your integration do not support that the WSDL has a method extended with new 
properties; normally this should be fine as long as you rebuild your integrations according to the 
new WSDL. 
- 
Some minor backwards-incompatible changes, but these are documented with the “From/Before 
DLL version X” notes under the affected service method properties. 
1.4 Overview model 
This model is a quick overview over the different services and methods that are available in the SIF API. All 
services are available in SOAP (on prem)/SOAP bridge and RPC (cloud/on prem), and the blue ones are 
available as REST (on prem) as well.

2 Usage 
 
2.1 Endpoint method types 
 
Four main types of methods are provided: 
- 
Synchronize methods - Updates or creates depending on whether they exist or not. 
- 
Create methods 
- 
Update methods 
- 
Get methods - Gets entities based on search criteria. 
 
2.2 SOAP Endpoints (on prem) 
Note: SIF SOAP API's support has been deprecated from 360 v5.9. SOAP Bridge (wrapper for 
WCF) with RPC support has been added with v5.9 and this wrapper only available from v5.9 
– v5.13. 
Every service documented in this document is implemented as SOAP web service endpoints, which are 
available for 360° on premise. 
There are multiple web service URL’s. The exact URL depends on the server configuration and load 
balancing, but in most cases they will be something like 
 
http://[servername]:4000/SI.WS.Core/SIF/ContactService.svc 
.. or CaseService.svc, DocumentService.svc, UserService.svc, FileService.svc 
 
WSDL’s for these services can be provided along with this document. WSDL specifies the functionality of 
a web service and can be used to build the client code.  
 
The default service endpoints use Soap 1.1, conform to WS-I Basic Profile 1.2, and use Basic 
authentication. Each service (apart from FileService) also has alternative endpoints with different 
configurations. These are accessed by adding a postfix to the URL.

2.4 RPC Endpoints for 360° Online (cloud) 
Note: It is always good practice to send authkey in request headers or authorization headers. We 
appreciate if external third parties send authkey into headers due to security reason. 
1. Request header 
 
2. Authorization 
 
 
 
All SOAP endpoints and operations have been rewritten as RPC endpoints to be able to use SIF on 360° 
Online. Note that the RPC endpoints can also be available in 360° on-prem installations, from 360° 
version 5.0. 
You will get a URL and a secret authorization key that you need to use to call the service. The DNS name 
and auth key will differ from customer to customer.  
The URL is: 
https://[customer specific url]/Biz/v2/api/call/SI.Data.RPC/SI.Data.RPC/[service]/ 
[operation]?authkey=[auth key] 
If you are moving from the SOAP to the RPC endpoints, please refer to the document “SIF API in 360° 
Online.pdf”.

2.4.5 Common error messages in SIF RPC 
 
Index was outside the bounds of the array 
Response:

{"Type":"Error","CorrelationId":"a70a91e7-79e1-42fd-9f06-
381a61e64e9c","ExceptionType":"IndexOutOfRangeException","Message":"Index was outside the 
bounds of the array.","Details":null} 
Cause: Wrong name of the operation in the call. This is case sensitive, e.g. you will get this error if you try 
to call Createcase instead of CreateCase 
 
Missing argument ‘...’ 
Response:  
{"Type":"Error","CorrelationId":"0fb835f9-9661-43f4-9328-
ca63e4c5c274","ExceptionType":"InvalidOperationException","Message":"Missing argument 
'…'","Details":null} 
Cause: You are missing a field in the body. This is also case sensitive. 
 
401 Unauthorized: Unable to authorize request 
Cause: There’s an error in the SIF configuration, and the problem is the user the service is running as. 
Contact Tieto.  
 
200 OK: Can not inititate ADContextUser because the context user is empty or invalid 
Cause: There is something wrong with the user in the ADContextUser field in the parameter – either the 
user is not a valid 360° user, or the format of the user’s ID is wrong.  
2.5 SOAP Bridge (360° 5.9 – 5.13) 
With version v5.14, we have deprecated the support for SOAP Bridge. Customers need to convert 
their integrations to SIF RPC before upgrading to v5.14. Please contact Tieto Public360 support for 
more information. 
From 360° version 5.9, we have provided the support for old SIF SOAP based services with RPC way of 
calling called SOAP bridge by introducing a new endpoint “SIF SOAP” under web admin -> Endpoint 
configuration just like “SIF RPC”. This bridge component is implemented as a Asp.net REST service but will 
translate the SOAP message and call the existing implementation. This new SOAP bridge will be available 
only on limited 360° versions, going further we will discontinue the SOAP services. 
You will get a URL and a secret authorization key that you need to use to call the service. The DNS name 
and auth key will differ from customer to customer.  
The URL is:

https://[customer specific url]/ Biz/v2-pbr/soapbridge/sif/[service]/?authkey=[auth key] 
2.5.1 Endpoint configuration 
You can configure through web admin (Please note that to add new endpoint, you should have 
“TechnicalAdministrator” role) 
 
2.5.2 Things to remember 
As this wrapper is very light weight and simple. There might be chances that for some scenarios it 
won’t work, especially for stream objects, where we deal with file operations (GetFile, CheckIn and 
CheckOut, Upload). For those operations (File related operations), it is highly recommended to use 
SIF RPC instead. 
2.6 Client responsibility 
A client/consumer, for example external systems, is responsible for the following: 
 
- 
Creating cases, documents and contacts in the correct order. This means that the service 
methods that are available must be called in the correct logical order. For example; you 
cannot create a document without first creating the case that it belongs to. 
- 
Files must be in the correct format. PDF files is not verified to be PDF/A. No file format 
validation is done by the service. 
- 
As a general rule, it is the client’s responsibility to validate and clean input to its own needs. 
Some validation is still done by the 360° business logic, but errors will be in the stack trace 
format thrown by the business logic. 
o 
Take note that customer specific validation can be implemented as a customer 
specific validation component. 
 
2.7 PDF Converting 
 

--- PAGE 28 ---
 
SIF APIs - Documentation 
 
16 Feb 2026
Public
 
© Tieto 
sif apis - documentation.docx 
Page 28 of 395 
 
Converting of files to PDF (PDF/A) is not done by the service. This should be done using the 360° Format 
Converter module which uses a third-party application for the conversion. How the conversion is done is 
best explained with an example: 
 
1. A client calls the service CreateDocument() or UpdateDocument() with files. 
2. The service creates/updates a document and adds files to it in 360. 
3. The 360° Format Converter module has a timer job that copies files to a third-party programs 
TODO folder. Only files that fit preconfigured requirements are copied to this folder, a typical 
requirement is that the file’s document status is Closed. 
4. The third-party application converts the files in the TODO folder. The files are then uploaded to 
360° to the correct document.  
5. The result is a 360° document with the original file(s) and PDF file(s). 
 
The service has, in other words, no responsibility of converting files to PDF. The third-party application 
module is PixEdit which supports a wide range of file formats for conversion.  
 
2.8 Authentication and ADContextUser 
Note: Usage of ADContextUser is not recommended at least with SIF RPC API. Good practice is to 
have dedicated integration/service user for authentication. Each SIF RPC endpoint will have 
dedicated service user, therefore no need to send a user explicitly in request. If any need to run the 
request on behalf of then please contact Public360 support. 
2.8.1 ADContextUser (not recommended) 
The ADContextUser property is supported by all the service methods and contains the login name of a 
valid 360° user. If provided, the operation will execute as (impersonate) this user. If not provided, the 
operation will execute as the user that has authenticated against the web service, or for RPC, the user that 
has been configured to run the service in the installation.  
Only some users can impersonate other users. For instance, if the client authenticates against the service 
with the user “domain\administrator”, and specifies “domain\user1” as ADContextUser, 
“domain\administrator” must have impersonation rights in 360°. This is specified in the configuration file 
of the service.  
Typical scenario 1: The client of the service is Application X. An integration user is created for this 
application, “domain\appx_int”, which is configured with impersonation rights. The client authenticates 
with “domain\appx_int”, but provides the login of the end user, “domain\user1”, as ADContextUser. The 
operation is then executed as user1. For instance, if a case is created, user1 will be logged as the user 
who created it.  
Typical scenario 2: Same as 1, except no ADContextUser is provided. In this case, the operation will be 
executed as appx_int. If a case is created, appx_int will be logged as the user who created it.  
It is recommended that a dedicated user is created either for all clients to use in common 
(“domain\360sif_int”), or for each of them to use separately (“appx_int”, “appy_int”, ..). If ADContextUser 
is provided, it’s enough with one common user. If ADContextUser is not provided, it is recommended to 

--- PAGE 29 ---
 
SIF APIs - Documentation 
 
16 Feb 2026
Public
 
© Tieto 
sif apis - documentation.docx 
Page 29 of 395 
 
have a user for each client, so that the 360° log shows where the data came from. For instance, if both 
Application X and Application Y create contacts in 360, it is useful to know which of them created a 
specific contact. The integration user should have the Registrar role or the All Access role. If it is going to 
synchronize users, it also needs the User Administrator role. 
 
2.8.2 Endpoint Authentication 
Type of authentication provided by endpoint config: 
Securing endpoints with bearer tokens adds an important extra layer of security by offloading the 
ownership of the secrets from the application (us) to the customer. Also note that the AuthProxy only 
validates tokens and keys - it does not play any part in the flow to acquire the token! 
2.8.2.1 Authkey - Anonymous  
Endpoints configured with “Authkey” will get dedicated service user with all access to 360. There is an 
option to select existing service user while configuration. All the actions will be performed by this service 
user. This user will not follow the same access control system as any other 360 users. 
You will get all necessary loggings for create and edit but not read. 
2.8.2.2 Bearer - (JWT)  
Authorization Code Grant (ACG) 
Securing an endpoint with this authentication type when client application running the requests with user 
context. Same users are also in 360. Using this type, you don’t have to worry about the access controls. 
What the user get access to in 360 is then controlled only by the internal 360 access control system. (360 
access matrix). 
You will also get all necessary loggings including read objects. 
 
Note: If a user’s have access to endpoint A secured with only bearer, they will also have access to 
endpoint B secured with bearer. The reason for this is that there is no way to separate access to different 
endpoints. To distinguish between two bearer type endpoints. Use ClientId property while configuring the 
endpoints. 
2.8.2.3 Combined User 
Bearer (JWT) + Authkey (Authorization Code Grant in combination with Authkey ACGK) 
This adds an extra dimension to the authentication by requiring a secret that is managed by the 360 
product (responsible). The resources are required to have a "technical administrator role" in 360 and is 
typically different from the resources managing the token configuration. The authkey is also adding "high 
level" authorization by restricting access to specific functionality in 360 using endpoint paths. 
You will also get all necessary loggings including read objects.

2.8.2.4 Combined Daemon  
Client Credential Grant in combination with Authkey (CCGA) 
Client credential grant cannot be used by its own without introducing additional information to identify 
the different clients: 
https://docs.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/active-directory/develop/v2-oauth2-client-creds-grant-flow 
This is the option to use when the client is acting on behalf of the user or as a separate application 
(daemon). The main "problem" here is that there is no user connected to the token. This means that you 
must use some "property" on the token as identification, or as we do, connect the token explicitly to a 360 
user. The coupling of the token and user is done on a given endpoint with a given authkey. 
Best practices. 
- 
Always try to create endpoint with “Bearer authentication” if you want to apply same access 
rule as 360 UI, User based authentication. 
- 
With bearer authentication, you will get all necessary logging in 360 for example, File read by 
“this user”. 
- 
If you want to have dedicated user for integration, it is possible to have that user in AD and 
360 with necessary/limited access rights. 
- 
If you are using “Authkey” for authentication, it is recommended to have it in headers instead 
of URL. 
- 
If you using “ClientId” property while configuring the endpoint, it is must that you send this 
information in headers along with required tokens. 
2.9 Contact identifiers 
There are three types of registered contacts in 360: Enterprises (/companies/departments), private 
persons and contact persons. A contact person is associated with an enterprise, a private person is not. A 
user always has a contact person, which contains the contact metadata of that user.  
In the SIF API, contacts are identified by their PersonalIdNumber (social security number or 
“fødselsnummer” in Norwegian) if they’re private persons, EnterpriseNumber (organization number or 
“organisasjonsnummer” in Norwegian) if they’re enterprises, or ExternalId if they’re contact persons. For 
instance, if you’re synchronizing a private person with PersonalIdNumber 1234567890, the service will 
look for a contact with that id and update it if it exists. 
When multiple external systems work with 360° contacts, or when there are existing contacts that should 
be referred to / updated by the service, care must be taken to prevent duplicates and other problems. 
This should be analyzed before the system is put into production. 
 
2.9.1 Creating contact person and private person by same external id 
Suppose If I am creating the private person with external id: “Test External Id 001” after that creating 
contact person with same external id i.e. “Test External Id 001” so, it will update the existing external id 

--- PAGE 31 ---
 
SIF APIs - Documentation 
 
16 Feb 2026
Public
 
© Tieto 
sif apis - documentation.docx 
Page 31 of 395 
 
with [duplicate] and append GUID at last for that external id. The existing updated external id look likes 
“[duplicate] Test External Id 001 e1a72269-e501-4a45-9da2-9586d1d6f78c” and previously created 
private person is deactivated. 
After that if you want to update anything against that external id it will update the newly created contact 
person’s entries not the older private person because that private person is deactivated now, and the 
external id is also updated 
And vice versa (same things are applicable when we are doing the same process for contact person to 
private person) 
 
Why this happens: 
External id is a unique field. So, we can’t use same external id for both private person and contact person. 
To handle this constraint, we are updating the existing external id with [duplicate] and append GUID at 
last, if we used the already exist external id while creating new contact person or a private person. 
 
2.10 Validation of personal id number and enterprise number 
When synchronizing private persons and enterprises, the service can optionally validate that the values 
comply with Norwegian fødselsnummer and organisasjonsnummer formats (11 and 9 digits and satisfying 
a modulus 11 validation).  
This functionality is toggled by the “SI.Data.WS.ValidateContactId” appSetting in the SIF web.config file. 
By default, it is set to false. 
 
 
2.11 Code values 
Some methods accept input in the form of code table values. For instance, when creating a case, you can 
set its Status property to a value from the Case status code table, such as “B”.  
 
All properties that accept code values can alternatively accept the recno of the code, on the format 
“recno:123”. It is less risky to specify the code by recno, since it’s possible (but rare) for code values to 
change.  
 
Read more about this in the Common Code Table: values chapter.

2.12 Property tags 
2.12.1 (*) 
This tag behind a property name means that the property is mandatory. 
 
2.12.2 (**) 
This tag behind a property name appears in pair with another property and means that one of the two 
properties are mandatory. You can choose the one that best fits your needs. 
 
2.12.3 (\) 
This tag behind property name means that at least one of properties with this symbol needs to be 
supplied.  
 
2.13 Remark Validation. 
In 360° version SP8 and below, remark title is mandatory. In version SP9 and above, remark content is 
mandatory. To support both scenarios, only one of the fields are now mandatory. Either remark title or 
remark content is mandatory, so that the remark validation is version independent. The value of remark 
title is assigned to remark content when content is null (not provided), and vice versa – the value of 
remark content is assigned to title, if title is not provided. 
3 Delta support 
Delta Support in an API (Using LastDate as Modified Date) 
Delta support in an API is a mechanism that allows clients to fetch only the data that has changed 
(created, updated, or deleted) since a specific point in time. This is efficient for syncing data without 
retrieving everything repeatedly. 
3.1 Scenario Overview 
- 
Input: A LastDate (datetime) is provided in the request. This represents the last time the client 
fetched data. 
- 
Response: 
- 
A list of records modified after LastDate. 
- 
A NextDeltaLastDate value – the new timestamp clients should use in the next delta 
request. 

--- PAGE 33 ---
 
SIF APIs - Documentation 
 
16 Feb 2026
Public
 
© Tieto 
sif apis - documentation.docx 
Page 33 of 395 
 
3.2 How It Works 
Request: 
{ 
    "LastDate":"2025-05-08T13:53:30.877", 
    "MaxReturnedCases":10, 
    "ArchiveCode":"00" 
} 
 
API Behaviour: 
- 
Query the database for records where ModifiedDate > LastDate. 
- 
Return those records in the response. 
- 
Calculate and return the highest ModifiedDate among the returned records as 
NextDeltaLastDate. 
 
Response Example: 
{ 
    "Cases": [ 
{},{},{} 
], 
    "TotalPageCount": 1, 
    "TotalCount": 3, 
    "NextDeltaLastDate": "2025-05-20T08:18:05.193", 
    "Successful": true, 
    "ErrorMessage": null, 
    "ErrorDetails": null 
} 
3.3 How the Client Should Use It  
- 
On the next request, the client should use NextDeltaLastDate as the new LastDate. 
- 
This ensures no duplicate or missed data. 
3.4 Important Considerations 
- 
ModifiedDate Field: Must be reliably updated on every insert/update. 
- 
Time Precision: Timestamps should include milliseconds to avoid collisions. 
- 
No Changes Case: If no data has changed, return an empty array and set NextDeltaLastDate = 
LastDate. 
3.5 Benefits of Delta Support 
- 
Reduces bandwidth usage. 
- 
Speed up the sync/retrieval operations. 
- 
Makes API usage scalable for mobile and distributed clients.

4.1 SynchronizeContactPerson 
 
Creates or updates a contact person contact in 360° based on ExternalId. If the supplied ExternalId does 
not exists in 360° the contact is created. If the supplied ExternalId exists in 360° the contact is updated to 
mirror the supplied information.   
 
In the scenario where there exist several contacts with the same ExternalId the oldest (lowest recno) will 
be updated, while an error message is logged to TraceView and the Windows Event Viewer. It is not 
possible that this web service method itself creates a duplicate of a contact, given that the ExternalId is 
correct. 
 
OBS: If a property is not set, meaning it is blank or null; the corresponding value in 360° will be set to an 
empty value. This behavior is unique to the contact related synchronization methods.

4.4 UpdateContactPerson 
 
This is a pure update operation for contact person contacts in 360° to provide Recno and ExternalID as a 
unique identifier.  
 
Primarily, only one of these IDs must be used.  
 
If the supplied ID does not exist in 360°, an error message is returned. If the supplied ID exist in 360°, the 
contact is updated only with the supplied information.   
 
OBS: If a property is not set, meaning it is blank or null; the corresponding value in 360° will not be set to 
an empty value (like in the Synchronize operations). It will keep its original value (set before the Update 
method is executed).

FirstName

4.5 UpdatePrivatePerson 
This is a pure update operation for private person contacts in 360° to provide Recno as a unique identifier 
(in addition to PersonalIdNumber and ExternalID).  
Primarily, only one of these IDs must be used. However, it is possible to use Recno in combination with 
PersonalIdNumber and/or ExternalID. Whenever a value on either PersonalIdNumber or ExternalID, or 
both, are sent in together with a value for Recno, then Recno is used as the identifier, and the provided 
values in PersonalIdNumber and/or ExternalID are added/updated in their respective fields in 360°. 
Therefore, this operation can be used to add and update the IDs of contacts in 360°.   
If the supplied ID does not exist in 360°, an error message is returned. If the supplied ID exist in 360°, the 
contact is updated only with the supplied information.   
In the scenario where there exists several contacts with the same PersonalIdNumber or ExternalID, an 
error message is returned telling that duplicate contacts exist. It is not possible that this web service 
method itself creates a duplicate of a contact, given that the PeronalIdNumber and ExternalID are correct. 
OBS: If a property is not set, meaning it is blank or null; the corresponding value in 360° will not be set to 
an empty value (like in the Synchronize operations). It will keep its original value (set before the Update 
method is executed). 
4.5.1 Input 
 
Data Contract: UpdatePrivatePersonParameter

360° field

ADContextUser 
- 
Read Authentication and ADContextUser 
FirstName

MiddleName

Middle name of the contact, concatenated to 
FirstName. 
LastName 
Contact – Name3 
 
Recno(**) 
Contact – Recno 
The recno of the contact used as a unique 
identifier for the contact in 360°, instead of 
personal identity number or external id number.  
 
If it exists, the contact is updated.

4.7 GetContactPersons 
 
Retrieves contact persons from 360° based on criteria defined in the input parameter. At least one criteria 
(Name, Recno, Categories or ExternalId) must be given. Returns a list of matching contact persons.  
 
4.7.1 Input 
 
Data Contract: GetContactPersonParameter 
 
Input Property 
360° field 
Comment 
ADContextUser 
- 
Read Authentication and ADContextUser 
Recno 
Contact – Recno 
Recno (unique identifier in 360) of the contact. 
Must be an exact match to return a contact. 
Name 
Contact – SearchName 
Name of the contact. Returns all contacts with a 
name that contains the search name. Example: 
“Inge” will return “Knut Inge N.” and “Inge Larsen” 
ExternalId 
Contact – ExternalID 
External identity number (unique identifier) of the 
contact. Must be an exact match to return a 
contact. 
Active 
Contact – 
ActiveContact 
Active/inactive contacts. Boolean. 
Categories

List of categories, identified by their code name in 
the configured language, or by recno on the 
format “recno:123”.

Contact - CreatedDate 
See Data Contract: DateRange for details.

-

to get more accurate result.

-

SortCriterion 
-

- 
RecnoDescending 
- 
RecnoAscending 
Note: Always use SortCriterion along with page, 
to get more accurate result.

- 
Boolean value, set true if you want additional 
360° fields which are not currently included in the

details.

4.10 Sub Contracts 
This chapter defines data contracts in use by the main data contracts of the Contact methods. 
 
4.10.1 Data Contract: Address 
 
Property 
360° field 
Comment 
StreetAddress 
Address - Address 
Example: “Drammensveien 1” 
ZipCode 
Address - ZipCode 
Zip code of address. 
ZipPlace 
Address - ZipPlace 
Zip place (City) of address. 
Country(*) 
Address - Country 
Country of address. To create an address the user has to 
include “Country” together with another field. E.g. just sending 
in “Country” will not create an address, but sending in Country 
and StreetAddress will create an address.  
 
Example of input formats: “Norway” or “NOR”. 
From version 5.10.164, added support for ISO 3166-1 apha-2 
country codes. 
County 
Address - County 
Municipality of address. Norwegian: “Kommune”.  
 
Examples of input formats: “BODØ” or “1804”, it is 
recommended that the number format is used, i.e. “1804”. 
Area 
Address - Area 
County of address. Norwegian: “Fylke”.  
 
Examples of input formats: “NORDLAND” or “18”, it is 
recommended that the number format is used, i.e. “18”. 
State 
Address – State 
Very optional, only used for some foreign countries.

4.10.5 Data Contract: DateRange 
Property 
360° field 
Comment 
From 
 
Greater than or equal to, can be precise down to seconds, not 
milliseconds. 
To 
 
Less than or equal to, can be precise down to seconds, not 
milliseconds. 
 
If either From or To is null, the search will not be limited by the lower (From) or upper (To) date. If both is 
null and exception is thrown, as this is not correct use. Then the DateRange object itself should be null.  
 
Take note that both From and To cannot be used to limit the search down to milliseconds difference, due 
to internal limitations. This means if you have two entities: 
 
Entity Object 1 – Date 2017.01.20 19:03:20:500 
Entity Object 2 – Date 2017.01.20 19:03:20:502 
 
Then you cannot limit the search by specifying that you want all documents created after the 501th ms to 
only get Entity Object 2, instead all search results will include both objects.

4.11 Get Contact Custom Data 
To get additional 360° fields, which are not currently, included in the webservice output objects.  
Follow the below steps: 
1. Open 360 admin and go to CONFIGURATION => SETTINGS and edit value for “SIF Contact Custom 
Fields Query” which will include metaquery for getting the custom field, for e.g. 
 
 
<QUERYDESC ENTITY="Contact" NAMESPACE="SIRIUS" DATASETFORMAT="XML"> 
      <RESULTFIELDS> 
            <METAITEM TAG="CF_testfield">CF_testfield</METAITEM> 
      </RESULTFIELDS> 
  <CRITERIA> 
        <METAITEM NAME="Recno" OPERATOR="IN"> 
          <VALUE>{0}</VALUE> 
        </METAITEM> 

--- PAGE 71 ---
 
SIF APIs - Documentation 
 
16 Feb 2026
Public
 
© Tieto 
sif apis - documentation.docx 
Page 71 of 395 
 
  </CRITERIA> 
</QUERYDESC> 
CF_testfield is the custom meta field in Contact Entity. 
Note: Entity name should start with capital letter. It returns Single value field only. 
2. The result will be displayed in <AdditionalFields> .. <AdditionalFields> section in the as follows: 
 
<AdditionalFields> 
            <AdditionalFieldParameter> 
                 <Name>CF_testfield</Name> 
                 <Value>TestCustomField</Value> 
            </AdditionalFieldParameter> 
      </AdditionalFields>

Typical REST URL 
Not Implemented Yet

https://[customer specific 
domain]/Biz/v2/api/call/SI.Data.RPC/SI.Data.RPC/UserService 
Typical SOAP 
Bridge URL 
(deprecated with 
v5.14)

pbr/soapbridge/sif/UserService 
 
5.1 SynchronizeUser 
 
Creates or updates a user in 360, identified by its login name. If the user does not exist, it is created. If it 
exists, it is updated to match the input. 
 
A user must be connected to an existing 360° contact person, so always call SynchronizeContactPerson 
in the contact service first.  
 
With version 5.14, new feature toggle (Feature name: SIFImprovedUserAuditTrail) has been introduced to 
limit the unnecessary logging for UserProfile when there is no change in UserProfile object since last 
update. 
To enable this feature toggle, contact Tieto P360 team. 
Note: This feature has been removed from version 6.0 
Points to consider: 
- 
Manually added profiles will get deactivated with SynchronizeUser unless those profiles are 
set with flag “ad lock” or mentioned in settings “SI.Data.User.Profiles.Ignore” 
- 
SynchronizeUser will respect the source user base.

5.2 GetUsers 
 
Retrieves users from 360° based on criteria defined in the input parameter, input parameter can be 
empty. Possible criterias (UserId,ContactExternalId,ContactRecno or none). Returns a list of matching 
users.  
 
5.2.1 Input 
 
Data Contract: GetUsersParameters 
 
Input Property 
360° field 
Comment 
ADContextUser 
 
Read Authentication and ADContextUser 
UserId 
User - UserId 
The ID that identifies the User (typically "domain\user"). 
ContactExternalId 
 
Search users associated with the given contact external id 
ContactRecno 
 
Search users associated with the given contact recno 
Page 
- 
Results are always returned paginated, meaning if your search 
results in 3000 objects, and MaxRows is 1000, then: 
Page 0 will have object 1-1000 
Page 1 will have object 1001-2000, etc. 
 
First page is 0, meaning 0-indexed. Defaults to 0. 
 
Only applies when UserId, ContactExternalId or ContactRecno 
are not provided. 
Note: Always use SortCriterion along with page, to get more 
accurate result. 
MaxRows 
- 
Read Page property for details. Defaults to all. 
 

--- PAGE 76 ---
 
SIF APIs - Documentation 
 
16 Feb 2026
Public
 
© Tieto 
sif apis - documentation.docx 
Page 76 of 395 
 
Only applies when UserId, ContactExternalId or ContactRecno 
are not provided. 
SortCriterion 
(v5.14 onwards) 
- 
Sort the search, is applied before Page and MaxRows 
limitations. Defaults to no sorting criterion, the database then 
controls the order. 
 
Enum values:  
- 
RecnoDescending 
- 
RecnoAscending 
Note: Always use SortCriterion along with page, to get more 
accurate result. 
 
5.2.2 Output 
 
Data Contract: GetUsersResult 
 
Output Property 
360° field 
Comment 
Users 
User 
List of UserBase objects. See chapter “Data Contract - UserBase” 
for details. 
TotalCount 
- 
Total number of hits.  
TotalPageCount 
- 
Total number of pages. 
Successful 
- 
Will be true as long as no exception occurred during the 
execution. This means that successful will be true even if no 
enterprises matching the criteria was found.  
ErrorMessage 
- 
If successful is false, this contains a short error message 
ErrorDetails 
- 
If successful is false, this contains a full stack trace

6.1 SynchronizeEstate 
Creates or updates an estate in 360, identified by its External ID. If the estate does not exist, it is created. 
If it exists, it is updated to match the input.  
Estate type defaults to Estate/Eiendom (recno:1) if nothing else is provided.  
 
6.1.1 Input 
 
Data Contract: SynchronizeEstateParameter 
 
Input Property 
360° field 
Comment 
ADContextUser 
- 
Read Authentication and ADContextUser 
ExternalID(*) 
Estate – ExternalId 
The external id number of the estate must be 
used as a unique identifier for the estate in 360. 
If unique, a new estate is created, otherwise the 
estate is updated. 
 
If more than one external system use the web 
service, external id’s must be unique also 
between external systems. 
Type 
 
Estate – ToEstateType 
Points to the Estate type, identified by the code 
name in the configured language, or by recno 
on the format “recno:123”. 
 
Default: Estate (recno: 1) 
DataSource 
- 
DataSource is presented as a log entry under 
the Log-tab on the estate. The point is to track 
which systems have made updates to the 
estate. 
Address 
Estate – ToAddress 
The address of the estate. See fields needed for 
input under chapter: “Data Contract: Address”.

6.2 GetEstates 
 
Retrieves estates from 360° based on criteria defined in the input parameter. At least one criteria 
(ExternalId, Recno or Address) must be given. Returns a list of matching estates.  
 
6.2.1 Input 
 
Data Contract: GetEstatesParameter 
 
Input Property 
360° field 
Comment 
ADContextUser 
- 
Read Authentication and ADContextUser 
Recno 
Estate – Recno 
Recno (unique identifier in 360) of the estate. 
Must be an exact match to return an estate  
ExternalID 
Estate - ExternalId 
ExternalID (possible unique identifier) of the 
estate. Must be an exact match to return an 
estate. 
Address 
Estate – ToAddress 
The address of the estate. See fields needed for 
input under chapter: “Data Contract: Address”. 
Multiple estates can be returned here. 
 
Page 
- 
Results are always returned paginated, meaning 
if your search results in 3000 objects, and 
MaxRows is 1000, then: 
Page 0 will have object 1-1000 
Page 1 will have object 1001-2000, etc. 
 
First page is 0, meaning 0-indexed. Defaults to 0. 
Note: Always use SortCriterion along with 
page, to get more accurate result. 
MaxRows 
- 
Read Page property for details. Defaults to all.

SortCriterion 
-

MaxRows limitations. Defaults to no sorting 
criterion, the database then controls the order. 
 
Enum values:  
- 
RecnoDescending 
- 
RecnoAscending 
Note: Always use SortCriterion along with 
page, to get more accurate result.

Country

country codes

7 Case service 
 
Typical SOAP URL 
http://[servername]:4000/SI.WS.Core/SIF/CaseService.svc 
Typical REST URL 
http://[servername]:4000/SI.WS.Core/SIFREST/api/cases/ 
Typical RPC URL 
https://[customer specific 
domain]/Biz/v2/api/call/SI.Data.RPC/SI.Data.RPC/CaseService 
Typical SOAP 
bridge URL 
(Deprecated with 
v5.14) 
https://[customer specific domain]/Biz/v2-
pbr/soapbridge/sif/CaseService 
 
7.1 CreateCase 
 
Creates a case in 360° with the case type Case (“Sak/Noark Sak” in Norwegian). The parameters in the 
data contracts accept either code value of the respective code or recno if nothing else is specified. 
 
7.1.1 Input 
 
Data Contract: CreateCaseParameter 
 
Input Property 
360° field 
Comment 
ADContextUser 
- 
Read Authentication and ADContextUser 
Title(*) 
Case – Description 
 
UnofficialTitle 
Case – UnofficialTitle 
If not set, Title will be used. 

--- PAGE 91 ---
 
SIF APIs - Documentation 
 
16 Feb 2026
Public
 
© Tieto 
sif apis - documentation.docx 
Page 91 of 395 
 
ExternalId 
 
External ID for the case. 
   Id 
External Integration 
keys – ExternalId 
External id. String value. 
   Type 
External Integration 
Keys - ExternalSystem 
External id type. Not mandatory. If not 
provided, default type “SI WS Case” will 
be used. 
 
External id is unique within each type, so 
type can be used if cases are imported 
from more than one external system 
which use ExternalId. 
 
Should have a name to reflect system 
and entity, e.g. “Visma Case”. 
 
ExternalName 
External Integration 
Keys - ExternalName 
Name of the external key (optional) 
StartDate 
Case – StartDate 
Start date of the case progress. 
Defaults to the creation date of the case. 
Status 
Case – ToCaseStatus 
Code value or recno. View common 
values. 
 
Example of use: 
Status=”B”; sets the status to  
“Under behandling” 
 
ArchiveCode and ArchiveType needs to 
be supplied to set the status that closes 
the case. E.g. “A” – “Avsluttet”

Defaults according to the ADUserContext

ClosedDate 
Case – ClosedDate 
The date the case is closed. 
Should be used together with a closing 
status and is ignored otherwise. 
 
The following happens if the Status 
property is set to a closing status: 
- 
If this property is set, the case 
will gain this date as ClosedDate. 
- 
If this property is not set 360° 
automatically sets the 
ClosedDate to the current date. 
JournalUnit

SubArchive

ArchiveCodes

ClassCodeParameter” for details.  
FiledOnPaper 
Case – Paper 
Indicating (true/false) if the case is stored 
on paper. 
Keywords

List of keywords connected to the case. 
E.g. ["Keyword 1","Keyword 2"] 
  
If a keyword that does not exist in 360° is 
used, this is added to the keywords code 
table (the user needs Admin rights to 
create keywords).

7.2 UpdateCase 
 
Updates a case in 360. If the case has a closed status this operation can only be done by an archivist. 
 
If a property is set to null, meaning not provided in the parameter passed to the method, the property is 
not updated. To clear the field in 360° the property has to be explicitly set to “”. Only works for string 
properties. Archive codes supplied to this method is ignored and no changes to an archive code is 
possible once the case is created. 
 
7.2.1 Input 
 
Data Contract: UpdateCaseParameter 
 
Input Property 
360° field 
Comment 
ADContextUser 
- 
Read Authentication and ADContextUser 
CaseNumber(\)

The case number. This property will be 
used to determine which case will be 
updated. A case cannot have its case 
number updated. 
ExternalId(\) 
 
External id. This property will be used 
to determine which case will be

7.3 GetCases 
 
Retrieves a list of all cases fulfilling the criteria of all the nonblank properties of the supplied parameter. At 
least one search criterion parameter must be supplied. 
Note: Some properties are behind the feature toggle, please contact Tieto Public 360 support to 
enable/disable the feature 
7.3.1 Input 
 
Data Contract: GetCasesQuery 
Note: Use flags when it is required. Do not set all flags to true, it might hamper the performance. 
Some flags are default set to false, set to true explicitly in request parameter. For example, if you want to 
return CaseContacts in result, set “IncludeCaseContacts” to true. 
If searching with dates (CreatedDate or ModifiedDate..), recommendation is to use DateCriteria property.

7.3.3 Get Case Custom Data 
To get additional 360° fields, which are not currently included in the webservice output objects.  
Follow the below steps: 

--- PAGE 112 ---
 
SIF APIs - Documentation 
 
16 Feb 2026
Public
 
© Tieto 
sif apis - documentation.docx 
Page 112 of 395 
 
1. Open 360 admin and go to CONFIGURATION => SETTINGS and edit value for “SIF Case Custom 
Fields Query” which will include metaquery for getting the custom field, for e.g. 
 
 
<QUERYDESC ENTITY=”Case” NAMESPACE=”SIRIUS” DATASETFORMAT=”XML”> 
      <RESULTFIELDS> 
            <METAITEM TAG=”CF_testfield”>CF_testfield</METAITEM> 
      </RESULTFIELDS> 
  <CRITERIA> 
        <METAITEM NAME=”Recno” OPERATOR=”IN”> 
          <VALUE>{0}</VALUE> 
        </METAITEM> 
  </CRITERIA> 
</QUERYDESC> 
CF_testfield is the custom meta field in Case Entity. 
Note: Entity name should start with capital letter. It returns Single value field only. 
2. The result will be displayed in <CustomFields> .. <CustomFields> section in the CaseResult as 
follows: 
 
<CustomFields> 
            <AdditionalFieldParameter> 
                 <Name>CF_testfield</Name> 
                 <Value>TestCustomField</Value> 
            </AdditionalFieldParameter> 
      </CustomFields> 
 
7.3.4 Configure maximum documents to be return while getting case 
You can configure the maximum number of documents to be returned for the cases in CaseResult. The 
default is 100, due to performance. 

--- PAGE 113 ---
 
SIF APIs - Documentation 
 
16 Feb 2026
Public
 
© Tieto 
sif apis - documentation.docx 
Page 113 of 395 
 
To do that follow the below steps: 
 
1. Open 360 web admin and go to Configuration->Settings and look for “SIF 
MaxReturnedDocuments while searching case”. 
2. Edit the value (integer value like 10, 20). To return all documents, put -1. 
3. And save the settings. 
4. After saving new setting, do a cache refresh. 
7.4 Sub Contracts 
 
This chapter defines data contracts in use by the main data contracts of the Case methods. 
 
7.4.1 Data Contract: CaseContactParameter 
Note: 
1. ReferenceNumber is the PersonalIdNumber for a private person and EnterpriseNumber for an enterprise, 
so while connecting private person or enterprise to a case, make use of this field. ReferenceNumber is not 
used for contact persons.  
 
However, if you are defining recno to a contact in this field, using the format “recno:xxxx”, the contact 
could be either a private person, enterprise or contact person. Choose whichever ID field you like. The 
connection will be set correctly in either case.  
2. ExternalID is the unique identification number of a contact person or a private person or an enterprise. 
It’s therefore possible to connect contacts to cases using this field, independent of the contact domain 
(type). A proper connection between the case and contact will be set either way.  
 
If you are defining recno to the contact on the format “recno:xxxx”, you can use this field across all 
contact domains (be it a contact person, enterprise or private person). 
This logic was implemented to make our customers and third-party suppliers use whichever ID 
field they like, without needing to consider the domain/type of the contact. It also potentially 
eliminates the need for extra calls to the contact service, to retrieve Recno, ExternalId and/or 
ReferenceNumber before connecting contacts to cases. 
Note: When mentioning recno of P360 contact, if format is not as recno:xxxxx, there might be 
chances of matching with either PersonalId or ExternalId if one exists with same value as recno.

7.4.3 Data Contract: ClassCodeParameter 
 
Classification codes have a type and a value. The type refers to the code table Noark classification type. 
Most classification types use codes from the code table Noark classification code as values. Some use 
custom user-supplied values, for instance a personal identification number.

7.4.7 Data Contract: AdditionalFieldParameter 
 
This can be used for additional 360° fields which are not currently included in the webservice input 
objects. It can be used for creating and updating fields, and for searching within some ‘Get’ services. Both 
standard and custom (customer specific) fields can be set via this parameter, but there are limitations. 
 
What can be done: 
- 
Update single value fields 
 
What can not be done: 
- 
Multi value fields (see 6.4.8 Data Contract: AdditionalFieldListParameter for multi value fields) 
- 
Fields on referenced entities (such as ToSomething.SomeField) 
 
Fields that are connected as a relation to the entity in the meta model, can only be set in some cases, and 
if so using 360° recno values. 
 
This parameter should only be used after consulting with a Tieto employee or someone with knowledge 
of the 360° meta model. 
 
For the create and update operations: If the supplied Name does not exist in the customer database, the 
input is just ignored and no error is thrown. 
 
Property 
360° field 
Comment 
Name(*) 
- 
The name of the field. This name is the name in the 360° meta model 
(Not always the same as seen in 360° GUI.) 
 
Example: “CF_key” 
Value(*) 
- 
The string value to be set in the field. 
OperatorType 
 
Comparison operator, used only for “Get” services. Defaults to equal 
operator (=). 

--- PAGE 124 ---
 
SIF APIs - Documentation 
 
16 Feb 2026
Public
 
© Tieto 
sif apis - documentation.docx 
Page 124 of 395 
 
 
Possible values (Operator column): 
Operator 
Description 
= 
Equal 
!= 
Not equal 
LIKE 
Like, ‘%’ should be used as 
wildcard 
GT 
Greater than 
GT= 
Greater than or equal 
LT 
Less than 
LT= 
Less than or equal 
IS NULL 
Is null 
IS NOT NULL 
Is not null 
 
7.4.8 Data Contract: AdditionalFieldListParameter 
 
This can be used for additional 360° fields which are not currently included in the webservice input 
objects. It can be used for searching within some ‘Get’ services and certain insert and update services. 
With this field you can pass list of values for multi-value fields. Both standard and custom (customer 
specific) fields can be used via this parameter, but there are limitations - Fields on referenced entities 
(such as ToSomething.SomeField) can not be used for searching,  
 
Fields that are connected as a relation to the entity in the meta model, can only be used in some cases, 
and if so using 360° recno values. 
 
You can pass name of 360 field in Name attribute and list of string in Value attribute, for e.g. 
you can search for cases with CaseStatus in 4,5,6. As mentioned above you can only pass recnos in this 
scenario, since case status is a relation to case.

For REST service, you can separate two attributes Name and Value with pipe operator (‘|’) and Value by 
(‘,’) operator for e.g. CaseType|2,10 
 
If the supplied Name does not exist in the customer database, the input is just ignored and no error is 
thrown. 
 
Property 
360° field

-

Example: “CF_key” 
Value(*) 
- 
List of values. Input format for REST GET operations can be comma 
separated string (e.g. CaseType|2,10)

7.4.12 Data Contract: AdditionalRelations 
This functionality is available from 360° version 5.12 and SIF standalone version 2.1.6. 
 
This field is used to setup a “GET” query to fetch the data with custom or standard relations which are not 
provided in standard request parameter for Case and Documents only. 
With this you can pass the list of relation fields.

The fields "document date”, “dispatched date” and “journal date” are made available through this service. 
In Norway, these are document specific dates that have defined behavior in line with NOARK.  
That implies the following: 
 
1. Journal date is required to be after both dispatched date and document date. 
2. Dispatched date is required to be before journal date but after document date. 
3. Document date is required to be before journal date and dispatched date.

It is only possible to set a contactperson in 
the same enterprise hierarchy as the 
ADUserContext user as responsible, and 
the responsible needs to be a 360° user. 
 
Provide only ONE of the four possible 
fields to ID responsible person. 
ResponsiblePersonRecno 
Document – OurRef - 
Referencenumber 
Looks for a contact with Recno equal to 
the property input. 
ResponsiblePersonEmail 
Document – OurRef  
Looks for a contact with Email equal to the 
property input. 
ResponsiblePersonUserId 
Document – OurRef 
Looks for a contact with UserId equal to 
the property input  
(username, E.g. “no\name.name”) 
ResponsibleEnterpriseNumber 
Document – ToOrgUnit 
The enterprise number of the responsible 
enterprise. 
 
Looks for a contact with ExternalID first, 
then for one with reference number equal 
to the property input. 
 
If neither this nor the 
ResponsibleEnterpriseRecno property is 
set, the responsible enterprise will be set 
to the responsible person’s enterprise. If 
the responsible person is not set, the 
responsible enterprise will be set to the 
main enterprise of all the users. 
 
It is only possible to set the responsible 
enterprise to an enterprise in the same 
enterprise hierarchy as the ADUserContext 
user.

This happens if you try to create a document with a category that requires an external recipient. Typically 
the category “Dokument ut”/”Outbound document”. 
 
Solve this by sending a contact reference or unregistered contact as the Recipient role with the 
webservice method call. If you need to create a contact to reference, you can do this by creating/syncing 
a private person. 
 
8.1.3.2 The document type must have an external sender 
 
“(($((#AB094BFDD#))The document type must have an external sender$))” 
 
This happens if you try to create a document with a category that requires an external sender. Typically 
the category “Dokument inn”/”Incoming document”. 
 
Solve this by sending a contact reference or unregistered contact as the Sender role with the webservice 
method call. If you need to create a contact to reference, you can do this by creating/syncing a private 
person.

8.2 UpdateDocument 
 
Updates a document in 360. If a property is set to null, meaning not provided in the parameter passed to 
the method, the property is not updated. To be able to clear the field in 360° the property has to be set to 
“”. Only works for string properties. 
 
 
What is possible and not possible with this method: 
- 
It is not possible to change or delete files. 
- 
It is not possible to add a new version of files. 
- 
It is possible to add files to a document by supplying the Files and DocumentNumber property.

8.3 SignOffDocument 
 
Method to sign off a document with a response code and an optional note. 
While reply with document if you choose response code: 
1. Answered with outbound document then you can only relpy with outbound document to 
incoming document 
2. Signed off with new memo then you can only reply to internal memo with internal memo 
 
Response 
code 
(Recno) 
Response 
code (Code) 
Response code 
(Description) 
Rule 
1 
AO (ENU) 
BU (NOR) 
Answered with 
outbound document 
If using this response code, you may only refer to 
an outbound document in the ReplyDocument 
parameter. 
 
This means that the referred document must 
have a document category that is connected to 
the O – Outbound (recno: 2) journal document 
type.  
 
The document reffered in Document parameter 
must be an incoming document. 
2 
WM (ENU) 
NN (NOR) 
Signed off with new 
memo 
If using this response code, you may only refer to 
an internal memo in the ReplyDocument 
parameter. 
 
This means that the referred document/memo 
must have a document category that is 
connected to the Memo – Internal memo with 
follow-up (recno: 3) journal document type. 
 
The document reffered in Document parameter 
must also be an internal memo.

8.4 DispatchDocuments 
 
Method to start the dispatch process for one or several documents.  
 
All files on each document will be included, and it will be dispatched to all recipients.  
 
Dispatch channel used for each recipient will be the channel chosen by 360°. This can be the one set as 
default channel or the one set on the document contact when creating or updating the document (See 
Data Contract: DocumentContactParameter and Data Contract: UnregisteredContactParameter). 
 
If any of the documents fail, the operation will continue with the next document in the list. A list of result 
objects will be returned. NB: The result only indicates if the dispatch process was started successfully. To 
see if the dispatch was completed successfully – check status in 360°. 
 
With version 6.7, This method will first validate the contacts to see if they have all necessary information 
to be ale to start the dispatch workflow. 
 
8.4.1 Input 
 
Data Contract: DispatchDocumentsParameter 
 
Input Property 
360° field 
Comment 
ADContextUser 
 
Read Authentication and ADContextUser

The recno of the document number to dispatch. 
Only provide one of the id fields 
Recno/DocumentNumber. 
   DocumentNumber(\)

DocumentNumber 
The document number of the document to dispatch

8.5 GetDocuments 
 
Take note that this method in addition to getting meta data on documents; may also be used to get file 
meta data and the physical file(s) inside the document. At least one search criterion parameter must be 
supplied. 
8.5.1 Input 
 
Data Contract: GetDocumentsQuery 
Note: Use flags when it is required. Do not set all flags to true, it might hamper the performance. 
Some flags are default set to false, set to true explicitly in request parameter. For example, if you want to 
return DocumentContacts in result, set “IncludeDocumentContacts” to true. 
If searching with dates (CreatedDate or ModifiedDate..), recommendation is to use DateCriteria property.

8.5.3 Get Document Custom Data 
To get additional 360° fields, which are not currently, included in the webservice output objects.  
Follow the below steps: 
1. Open 360 admin and go to CONFIGURATION => SETTINGS and edit value for “SIF Document 
Custom Fields Query” which will include metaquery for getting the custom field, for e.g. 
 
<QUERYDESC ENTITY="Document" NAMESPACE="SIRIUS" DATASETFORMAT="XML"> 
<RESULTFIELDS> 
            <METAITEM TAG="CF_testfield">CF_testfield</METAITEM> 
 </RESULTFIELDS> 
 <CRITERIA> 
<METAITEM NAME="Recno" OPERATOR="IN"> 

--- PAGE 163 ---
 
SIF APIs - Documentation 
 
16 Feb 2026
Public
 
© Tieto 
sif apis - documentation.docx 
Page 163 of 395 
 
           <VALUE>{0}</VALUE> 
 </METAITEM> 
 </CRITERIA> 
</QUERYDESC> 
CF_testField is the custom meta field in Document Entity. 
Note: Entity name should start with capital letter. It returns Single value field only. 
2. The result will be displayed in <CustomFields> .. <CustomFields> section in the DocumentResult  
as follows: 
 
<CustomFields> 
     <AdditionalFieldParameter> 
 
<Name>CF_testfield</Name> 
 
<Value>TestCustomField</Value> 
     </AdditionalFieldParameter> 
</CustomFields> 
 
8.5.4 Configure the maximum files to return while getting document 
You can configure the maximum number of files to be returned for the documents in DocumentResult. The 
default number is 100, due to performance.  
To do that follow the below steps: 
 
1. Open 360 web admin and go to Configuration->Settings and look for “SIF MaxReturnedFiles 
while searching document”. 
2. Edit the value (integer value like 10, 20). To return all files, put -1. 
3. And save the settings. 
4. After saving new setting, do a cache refresh. 
 
8.5.5 Configure SQL query hints 
This functionality is available from 360° version 5.12 and SIF standalone version 2.1.9. 
In some scenarios if GetDocuments operation performs poorly, adding SQL query hints can help the issue. 
Most often this might be the case in on-premises environments and especially if the SQL database has 

--- PAGE 164 ---
 
SIF APIs - Documentation 
 
16 Feb 2026
Public
 
© Tieto 
sif apis - documentation.docx 
Page 164 of 395 
 
been upgraded to newer version at some point. There is functionality in 360° and SIF that makes it 
possible to add a query hint to the generated SQL query. The desired query hint needs to be added to two 
settings (Web admin  Configuration  Settings): 
1. SIF Get Documents Query Hint 
([360url]/locator/Admin/Setting/Details?Id=sif.getdocumentsqueryhint&standalone=true) 
o 
This is a single value field 
2. Query hint options 
([360url]/locator/Admin/Setting/Details?Id=sql.hintoptions&standalone=true) 
o 
This is a system-wide comma separated multivalue field that lists all the available query 
hints in the system. The query hint specified in step 1. needs to be included here as well. 
List of available query hints (for SQL 2019) can be found here: https://docs.microsoft.com/en-us/sql/t-
sql/queries/hints-transact-sql-query?view=sql-server-ver15#use_hint. 
 
8.6 Sub Contracts 
 
This chapter defines data contracts in use by the main data contracts of the Document methods. 
 
8.6.1 Data Contract: DocumentContactParameter 
Note: 
1. ReferenceNumber is the PersonalIdNumber for a private person and EnterpriseNumber for an enterprise, 
so while connecting private person or enterprise to a document, make use of this field. ReferenceNumber 
is not used for contact persons.  
 
However, if you are defining recno to a contact in this field, using the format “recno:xxxx”, the contact 
could be either a private person, enterprise or contact person. Choose whichever ID field you like. The 
connection will be set correctly in either case.  
2. ExternalID is the unique identification number of a contact person or a private person or an enterprise. 
It’s therefore possible to connect contacts to documents using this field, independent of the contact 
domain (type). A proper connection between the document and contact will be set either way.  
 
If you are defining recno to the contact on the format “recno:xxxx”, you can use this field across all 
contact domains (be it a contact person, enterprise or private person). 
This logic was implemented to make our customers and third-party suppliers use whichever ID 
field they like, without needing to consider the domain/type of the contact. It also potentially 
eliminates the need for extra calls to the contact service, to retrieve Recno, ExternalId and/or 
ReferenceNumber before connecting contacts to documents.

8.6.7 Data contract: CreateFileFromTemplateParameter 
This can be used to create new files, either for a new document or to an existing documents. The files will 
be created based on the template defined. It is not possible to set any other metadata when creating the 
file from template. Recno to the template can be found in the database or in the web administrator. All 
available templates can be set. 
If more than one file from template is defined, the first one will be added as Main Document and the other 
ones as Attachments. 
If file from template and uploaded files (Se Data Contract: File) are included, the file from template will 
always be added as Main Document. 
 
Property 
360° field 
Comment

8.6.9 Data Contract: ClassCodeParameter 
Classification codes have a type and a value. The type refers to the code table Noark classification type. 
Most classification types use codes from the code table Noark classification code as values. Some use 
custom user-supplied values, for instance a personal identification number.

ExternalId(\) 
 
The external id of the project you wish to 
retrieve. Search uses a combination of Id

   Id 
External Integration Keys 
– ExternalId 
External id of a project. 
   Type 
External Integration Keys - 
ExternalSystem 
If not provided, default value “SIF WS 
Project” will be used. This is the type set via 
CreateProject if type was not provided 
when importing a project. 
Title(\) 
Project – Description 
The title search is based on the SQL “LIKE” 
operator, so it is possible to search with 
wildcards. For instance, “%” can be used as 
a substitute for zero or more characters in 
the title search. 
 
If you define “%test%” for instance, a 
search for all projects containing “test” in 
the Title will be performed. 
 
It is also possible to search for exact 
matches, simply by excluding any wildcards 
in the Title parameter.  
CategoryCode(\) 
Project – Category 
The project category (code value) to the 
projects you want to retrieve. 
StatusCode(\) 
Project – Status 
The project status (code value) to the 
projects you want to retrieve. 
AdditionalFields(\) 
 
Searches using additional fields – see 
chapter for AdditionalFieldParameter for 
details, what kind of fields and how to use 
this parameter. 
Page 
- 
Results are always returned paginated, 
meaning if your search results in 3000 
objects, and MaxRows is 1000, then: 
Page 0 will have object 1-1000 
Page 1 will have object 1001-2000, etc.

9.3.3 Get Project custom data 
To get additional 360° fields, which are not currently, included in the webservice output objects.  
Follow the below steps: 
1. Open 360 admin and go to CONFIGURATION => SETTINGS and edit value for “SIF Project Custom 
Fields Query” which will include metaquery for getting the custom field, for e.g. 
 
<QUERYDESC ENTITY="Project" NAMESPACE="SIRIUS" DATASETFORMAT="XML"> 
<RESULTFIELDS> 
            <METAITEM TAG="CF_testfield">CF_testfield</METAITEM> 
 </RESULTFIELDS> 
 <CRITERIA> 

--- PAGE 192 ---
 
SIF APIs - Documentation 
 
16 Feb 2026
Public
 
© Tieto 
sif apis - documentation.docx 
Page 192 of 395 
 
<METAITEM NAME="Recno" OPERATOR="IN"> 
           <VALUE>{0}</VALUE> 
 </METAITEM> 
 </CRITERIA> 
</QUERYDESC> 
CF_testField is the custom meta field in Project Entity. 
Note: Entity name should start with capital letter. It returns Single value field only. 
2. The result will be displayed in <CustomFields> .. <CustomFields> section in the ProjectResult  as 
follows: 
 
<CustomFields> 
     <AdditionalFieldParameter> 
 
<Name>CF_testfield</Name> 
 
<Value>TestCustomField</Value> 
     </AdditionalFieldParameter> 
</CustomFields>

9.4.2 Data Contract: ProjectContactParameter 
Note: 
1. ReferenceNumber is the PersonalIdNumber for a private person and EnterpriseNumber for an enterprise, 
so while connecting private person or enterprise to a project, make use of this field. ReferenceNumber is 
not used for contact persons.  
 
However, if you are defining recno to a contact in this field, using the format “recno:xxxx”, the contact 
could be either a private person, enterprise or contact person. Choose whichever ID field you like. The 
connection will be set correctly in either case.  
2. ExternalID is the unique identification number of a contact person or a private person or an enterprise. 
It’s therefore possible to connect contacts to projects using this field, independent of the contact domain 
(type). A proper connection between the project and project will be set either way.  
 
If you are defining recno to the contact on the format “recno:xxxx”, you can use this field across all 
contact domains (be it a contact person, enterprise or private person). 
 
This logic was implemented to make our customers and third-party suppliers use whichever ID field 
they like, without needing to consider the domain/type of the contact. It also potentially eliminates 
the need for extra calls to the contact service, to retrieve Recno, ExternalId and/or ReferenceNumber 
before connecting contacts to projects. 
 
Input Property 
360° field 
Comment 
ReferenceNumber(\) 
ProjectContact – 
ToContact – 
Referencenumber  
The identification number of the contact. This is 
PersonalIdNumber for private persons and 
EnterpriseNumber for enterprises. 
 
Use either this field OR the ExternalId-field. 
 
If this contact doesn’t have a reference number, the 
format will be “recno:xxxx”. 
 
Note:

10 File service 
 
Typical SOAP URL 
http://[servername]:4000/SI.WS.Core/SIF/FileService.svc  
Typical REST URL 
http://[servername]:4000/SI.WS.Core/SIFREST/api/files/ 
Typical RPC URL 
https://[customer specific 
domain]/Biz/v2/api/call/SI.Data.RPC/SI.Data.RPC/FileService 
Typical SOAP 
Bridge URL  
(Deprecated with 
v5.14) 
https://[customer specific domain]/Biz/v2-pbr/soapbridge/sif/FileService 
 
Note: ADContextUser/User field is mandatory for File related methods (CreateFile, DeleteFile, UpdateFile, 
Upload, CheckOutAndGetFileForExternalControl, UploadAndCheckInFileFromExternalControl)  
 
10.1 Upload 
 
Streams a file to the 360° server, where it is stored temporarily, and should be referred to in 
CreateDocument() or UpdateDocument(). 
 
It should be noted that if neither the FileName (including extension) nor the FileFormat input 
parameter is provided to the Upload operation, the files created using this service will default to PR – 
Production format, regardless of the file format specified in the Format parameter (on the File data 
contract) in the Create- and UpdateDocument operations of the Document service. 
 
If a file format is defined in either FileName, FileFormat or both parameters – 360° will automatically 
handle and set the correct File Variant/Version format (Production format or Archive format) on the 
uploaded file. 
10.1.1 Input

16 Feb 2026
Public

sif apis - documentation.docx

Input Property 
Comment 
FileStream/ 
FileData 
The file data. FileStream is used in SOAP and FileData is used in RPC.  
NB! The FileStream should be of type Stream in SOAP and bytearray in RPC. 
User 
The uploaded file is stored in this users 360° cache. This user must be the same as 
the user you authenticate (ADUserContext) with when referring to the file, 
otherwise it will not be able to locate the it. 
FileName 
The file name. See note above regarding use. 
FileFormat 
The file format. See note above regarding use. 
 
10.1.2 Output 
 
Data Contract: UploadFileResult 
 
Output Property 
Comment 
Successful 
If true, the file was uploaded. 
FileReference 
Use this value to refer to the uploaded file. 
ErrorMessage 
If unsuccessful, this contains a short error message 
ErrorDetails 
If unsuccessful, this contains a full stack trace 
10.2 Upload large file 
To upload large file into 360 sytem using integrations, make use of the endpoint: 
/v2/support/Uploadfile 
10.2.1 Create Endpoint 
To add new endpoint through web admin, “TechnicalAdministrator” role is must. 
Go to 360 web admin, under Configuration -> Endpoint configuration

checked in with the same user and external system, so use this method in combination with 
UploadAndCheckInFileFromExternalControl

10.14 Get Files Custom Data 
To get additional 360° fields, which are not currently, included in the webservice output objects.  
Follow the below steps: 
1. Open 360 admin and go to CONFIGURATION => SETTINGS and edit value for “SIF File Custom 
Fields Query” which will include metaquery for getting the custom field, for e.g. 
 
 
<QUERYDESC ENTITY="File" NAMESPACE="SIRIUS" DATASETFORMAT="XML"> 
      <RESULTFIELDS> 
            <METAITEM TAG="CF_testfield">CF_testfield</METAITEM> 
      </RESULTFIELDS> 
  <CRITERIA> 
        <METAITEM NAME="Recno" OPERATOR="IN"> 
          <VALUE>{0}</VALUE> 
        </METAITEM> 
  </CRITERIA> 
</QUERYDESC> 
CF_testfield is the custom meta field in File Entity. 
Note: Entity name should start with capital letter. It returns Single value field only. 
2. The result will be displayed in <AdditionalFields> .. <AdditionalFields> section in the as follows: 
 
<AdditionalFields> 
            <AdditionalFieldParameter> 
                 <Name>CF_testfield</Name> 
                 <Value>TestCustomField</Value> 
            </AdditionalFieldParameter> 
      </AdditionalFields>

possible to configure a limitation of the roles in the relation to the case that should be returned in the 
response.

12.1.3 MyCases Config 
MyCases config setting value set as criteria when you pass OrganizationalId and PersonalId. 
Follow the below steps: 
1. Open 360 admin and go to CONFIGURATION => SETTINGS and edit value for “SIF MyCases 
Config” which will filter cases based on Case type and CaseRoles specified in this setting , for e.g. 
             {"data":[{"CaseType":"2","CaseRoles":[{"Role":"9"}]}]} 
“2” is the Case type recno of Code table: Case type. 
“9” is the Contact – Case Role recno of Code table Contact – Case Role. 
Note: You need to verify that ToCaseTypes is connected with CaseType: Case in Code table Contact – 
Case Role.

13.4 GetEvents 
This method makes it possible to return events based on 360 logs for the input specified. 
13.4.1 Input 
DataContract: GetEventsQuery 

--- PAGE 240 ---
 
SIF APIs - Documentation 
 
16 Feb 2026
Public
 
© Tieto 
sif apis - documentation.docx 
Page 240 of 395 
 
 
Input Property 
360° field 
Comment 
Recno 
Entitylog-Recno 
Recno of Entitylog 
LogCount 
- 
Number of log entries which are 
processed during one execution. 
e.g: Recno = 200001 and LogCount=10, 
all the events for logs recno 200001, 
200002, … , 200010.  
Note: Default value 100. 
Max value = 1000 
FromDate 
Entitylog- CreatedDate 
Log created date 
ToDate 
Entitylog- CreatedDate 
Log created date 
Note: Input fields should be used in combination/pair, 
1. (Recno – LogCount) 
If you want events on basis of Recno, then recno is mandatory. If Recno is present and LogCount 
is not set, then 100 will set by default. Upper limit for LogCount value = 1000 
2.  (FromDate – ToDate) 
If you want events between two dates then you can make use of this pair, both are mandatory. 
Validations:  
a. FromDate should not be greater than ToDate. 
b. ToDate should not be less than FromDate. 
3. You can not pass all four values to events, otherwise it will throw exception. 
4. You can set additional criteria to exclude the entity logs which you don't want while getting 
events. See the chapter GetEvent Setting for more details.

13.4.3 GetEvent Setting 
GetEventDynamicCriteria setting value set as criteria when you want to exclude logs while getting events. 
Follow the below steps: 
1. Open 360 admin and go to CONFIGURATION => SETTINGS and edit value for “SIF 
GetEventDynamicCriteria while getting event(s)” to excludes the logs of entity which will satisfied 
the criteria. Setting value will be in XML format. 
<Setting> 
<{EntityName}> 
<MetaFieldName> 
{value} 
</MetaFieldName> 
</{EntityName}> 
</Setting> 
e.g.: 

--- PAGE 242 ---
 
SIF APIs - Documentation 
 
16 Feb 2026
Public
 
© Tieto 
sif apis - documentation.docx 
Page 242 of 395 
 
<Setting> 
    <Case> 
      <CaseType> 
         2 
      </CaseType > 
    </Case> 
</Setting> 
 
EntityName: Case 
MetaFieldName: CaseType 
Value: 2 
 
In this way you can specify the criteria.

13.8.4 Get Classification Custom Data 
To get additional 360° fields, which are not currently included in the webservice output objects.  
Follow the below steps: 
1. Open 360 admin and go to CONFIGURATION => SETTINGS and edit value for “SIF 
Classifcation Custom Fields Query” which will include metaquery for getting the custom field, 
for e.g. 
 
 
<QUERYDESC ENTITY=”Noark Classification code” NAMESPACE=”SIRIUS” 
DATASETFORMAT=”XML”> 
      <RESULTFIELDS> 
            <METAITEM TAG=”CF_testfield”>CF_testfield</METAITEM> 
      </RESULTFIELDS> 
  <CRITERIA> 
        <METAITEM NAME=”Recno” OPERATOR=”IN”> 
          <VALUE>{0}</VALUE> 
        </METAITEM> 

--- PAGE 250 ---
 
SIF APIs - Documentation 
 
16 Feb 2026
Public
 
© Tieto 
sif apis - documentation.docx 
Page 250 of 395 
 
  </CRITERIA> 
</QUERYDESC> 
CF_testfield is the custom meta field in Case Entity. 
Note: Entity name should start with capital letter. It returns Single value field only. 
2. The result will be displayed in <CustomFields> .. <CustomFields> section in the 
GetClassificationsResult as follows: 
<CustomFields> 
            <AdditionalFieldParameter> 
                 <Name>CF_testfield</Name> 
                 <Value>TestCustomField</Value> 
            </AdditionalFieldParameter> 
      </CustomFields>

13.11 AutomaticArchivingEnabler SIF support (Under development) 
Do not use these methods before taking assistance from SIF team. These methods are still under 
development. Full documentation and implementation will release in future version. 
This new integration will archive the documents/cases from external system to P360. To be able to 
archive correctly to P360, external system should use new added endpoints 
(SupportService/BulkImportSync or SupportService/BulkImportAsync) and follow standard schema 
protocol.  
Note: This is still under development 
13.11.1 BulkImportSync (under development) 
With this method, you can archive documents or cases in bulk. In response you will receive information 
about archived entities synchronously. 
Note:  This is still under development 
13.11.2 BulkImportAsync (under development) 
With this method, you can archive documents or cases in bulk asynchronously. Bulk request will be stored 
in queue and will be processed later by nightly/scheduled job “Automatic Archiving job”.

13.13.2 Output 
On success, returns HTTP 201 with no content. 
On error, returns HTTP status based on error: 
- 
HTTP 403 – When user is not allowed to access the endpoint or given entity. 
- 
HTTP 400 – When request is not valid. 
- 
HTTP 500 – If there is an error while processing the request. 
On error, the following JSON content is returned: 
Data Contract: WSOperationResult 
Output Property 
360° field 
Comment 
Successful 
- 
Will always be false in error scenarios 
ErrorMessage 
- 
Contains a short error message 
ErrorDetails 
- 
Contains a full stack trace if stack trace is

13.16 TempEntityStorage Text Support (v5.18 onwards) 
With methods defined in subchapters, you can get, add/modify, and delete text content in the 
TempEntityStorage. The access to TempEntityStorage is limited to configured systems (owner), which are 
defined in setting SIF TempEntityStorage Config (v5.18). 
These functions are available from v5.18 onwards.

13.17 CreateHistoricLog (6.5) 
To support data migration and retain historic logs from other IT solutions, like Public 360, a new 
SupportService/CreateHistoricLog method is needed to be used in eArchive. 
Being able to import historic event logs from other sources, to preserve historic accuracy and improve 
eArchive as a flexible archive core for all markets.

14 Revision Service 
14.1 CreateRevision 
 
Creates New Revision with following input parameter.  Revision can be created with statuses like In 
progress, Completed, Void, In Revision, Approved, In approval process. At a time, there can be only one 
revision in “In Progress” status. If you try to create new revision with In progress and if it already have one 
with In Progress it will automatically makes the previous revision status as “Completed” and created new 
revision with “In Progress”.     
 
14.1.1 Input 
Data Contract: CreateRevisionParameter 
 
Input Property 
360° field 
Comment 
ADContextUser 
-

ToDocument(Recno) 
Recno (unique identifier in 360) of the 
Document.

Code Table - Version Status 
Description

enters while creating Revision.
