14.2 UpdateRevision 
 
Update existing Revision with following input parameter.                                                                      Revision Status 
– It can be updated if existing revision is in status “In Progress”,” In approval process “or “In Revision”. 
User cannot modify revision status if it has other status.  
Revision Description – User can update revision description anytime no criteria applied on it. 
These Revision fields could be updated depend on Document Number, Document Recno or Revision 
Recno. So, either DocumentRecno, DocumentNumber Or Revision Recno is mandatory while updating 
Revision.

14.3 GetRevision 
GetRevision will fetch data based on input parameters. Revision Data will be fetched based on 
DocumentNumber, DocumentRecno or RevisionRecno. 
GetRevisionInProgress and RevisionRecno parameters are either or. If GetRevisionInProgress is passed by 
user as input parameter then RevisionRecno will not be considered. 
If GetRevisionInProgress is set True, it will return Revision with status InProgress.If it is set False, it will 
return last closed Revision. 
RevisionRecno- If RevisionRecno is passed then GetRevisionInProgress will not be considered.  Revision 
data will be fetched based on revision Recno.

15 14. Activity Service 
Typical RPC URL 
https://[customer specific 
domain]/Biz/v2/api/call/SI.Data.RPC/SI.Data.RPC/ActivityService 
Get/Add/Update activities through SIF. 
Note: Currently, we support limited set of activities through this endpoint, read below for more 
information. 
Currently this service is available for 5.13,5.17 and 5.16 versions of 360. 
15.1 CreateWorkflowActivity 
Creates an activity in 360° with activity type category review sequential, review parallel and approval. 
The parameters in the data contracts accept either code value of the respective code or recno if nothing 
else is specified.

Documents

activity belongs to.

15.3.3 Get Activity Custom Data 
To get additional 360° fields, which are not currently included in the webservice output objects.  
Follow the below steps: 
1. Open 360 admin and go to CONFIGURATION => SETTINGS and edit value for “SIF Activity Custom 
Fields Query” which will include metaquery for getting the custom field, for e.g. 

--- PAGE 284 ---
 
SIF APIs - Documentation 
 
16 Feb 2026
Public
 
© Tieto 
sif apis - documentation.docx 
Page 284 of 395 
 
 
 
<QUERYDESC ENTITY=”Activity” NAMESPACE=”SIRIUS” DATASETFORMAT=”XML”> 
      <RESULTFIELDS> 
            <METAITEM TAG=”CF_testfield”>CF_testfield</METAITEM> 
      </RESULTFIELDS> 
  <CRITERIA> 
        <METAITEM NAME=”Recno” OPERATOR=”IN”> 
          <VALUE>{0}</VALUE> 
        </METAITEM> 
  </CRITERIA> 
</QUERYDESC> 
CF_testfield is the custom meta field in Case Entity. 
Note: Entity name should start with capital letter. It returns Single value field only. 
2. The result will be displayed in <CustomFields> .. <CustomFields> section as follows: 
 
<CustomFields> 
            <AdditionalFieldParameter> 
                 <Name>CF_testfield</Name> 
                 <Value>TestCustomField</Value> 
            </AdditionalFieldParameter> 
      </CustomFields>

It is only possible to set a

fields to ID responsible person.

to the property input. 
ResponsiblePersonEmail 
Activity – OurRef

the property input. 
ResponsiblePersonUserId 
Activity – OurRef  
Looks for a contact with UserId 
(username, E.g. “no\name.name”) 
ResponsibleEnterpriseNumber 
Activity – ToOrgUnit 
The enterprise number of the 
responsible enterprise. 
 
Looks for a contact with ExternalID 
first, then for one with reference 
number equal to the property input. 
 
If neither this nor the 
ResponsibleEnterpriseRecno property 
is set, the responsible enterprise will 
be set to the responsible person’s 
enterprise. If the responsible person is 
not set, the responsible enterprise will 
be set to the main enterprise of all the 
users. 
 
Provide only ONE of the two possible 
fields to ID responsible enterprise. 
ResponsibleEnterpriseRecno 
Activity – ToOrgUnit 
Looks up enterprise based on recno. 
Must be recno of a valid enterprise.

16 Ping methods 
 
Each service endpoint has a Ping() method that can be used to test that you have access to the service, 
that it is correctly installed, and that your user has at least read permissions to 360°. If it returns without 
throwing an exception, everything is ok. 
 
 
 

--- PAGE 307 ---

Public
 
© Tieto 
sif apis - documentation.docx 
Page 307 of 395 
 
17 Code Table - common values 
360° stores much of its configuration in Code Tables and Code Table values. These values are database 
rows that defines what values can be used as metadata for entities 360°. An example is Code Table: 
Document status, which is a list of all possible document statuses allowed and metadata related to that 
status. 
Consider the case of document status. Customers with a standard 360° installation usually have the same 
possible document statuses. However, some do not, and that is why we do not supplement an enum of 
available values. Some code table values is also different based on which country the customer is located 
in – e.g. Denmark may have different case types than Finland. So, any third party that integrates with 
360° should verify what values can be used. 
When calling a service method with an input that reference one of these code table values, you can do 
two things: 
- 
Input “recno:[recno_from_code_table]”. Example “recno:6”. 
- 
Sometimes only using the recno may work. Example “6”. But prefixing it with recno is 
recommended. 
- 
Input “[code_from_code_table]”: Example: “J”.  
 
Be warned, valid codes vary by configured web.config value for key SI.Data.BL.LanCode. Since code 
tables are localized with different code values for each language, it’s important to use codes from the 
right language. The default language for the SIF SOAP web service is Norwegian.

18 Support for Default Values 
This functionality enables SIF to have set of defined values for Case and Documents entities, which are 
used to create case and document with those values as default if not passed. 
18.1 Brief about Default values and its related tables in 360: 
18.1.1 What is Default value? 
A default value refers to the pre-existing value of a user-configurable setting that is assigned to a 
software application. 
They are standard values that are universal to an instance and intended to make entity as accessible as 
possible "out of the box" without necessitating a lengthy configuration process prior to use. 
The user add/update default values according to their personal preferences. 
18.1.2 Why it is required? 
It will make things simpler for the consumer to use the system. User don’t have to enter repetitive values 
every time.  
User don’t have any idea about internal structure of data in 360, so default value makes his life easy if he 
has these default value sets configured in system by integration systems or by someone for him.  
Once we set the values using default value it will get automatic populate to the forms or in integrations. 
No need to enter again and again. It will save time and makes the system more user friendly. 
18.1.3 Code table structure: 
This default value sets are structured in a hierarchy as defined below. 
 
Default Value
Default Value 
Template
Default Value 
Context
SIFDefaultValeSet1
SIFDFCaseTemplate1
Title
Status
SIFDFDocumentTemplate1
Status

sif apis - documentation.docx 
Page 314 of 395 
 
First, we should create default value context, then we can create default value template related to default 
value context after this we can create default values related to specific templates.  
A single default value context can be associated to multiple templates.  
A single template can be associated to multiple default values. 
 
The following code tables are used, listed in hierarchically order:  
• 
Code Table: DefaultContext: Top node. Consider this as a name of the Set. 
• 
Code Table: DefaultValueTemplate: Template that all default values are connected. 
• 
Code Table: DefaultValue: One row here equals one default value to an object. 
18.2 Usage of Default values: 
SIF uses Default Value Context and its values to set these values to input parameters for 
CreateCaseParameter or CreateDocumentParameter.  
To use this feature, we need set name of Default Value Context to CreateCaseParameter or 
CreateDocumentParameter property named as ‘DefaultValueSet’. Once this property is set with default 
value context name, the DefaultValueApplier in SIF validates properties of input parameter with Default 
value set and updates those values for respective properties which are null or empty. You must then call 
the API in the usual way. 
18.3 Creating new Default Value sets 
You can configure these sets by mmc 360 Red admin UI. 
There is an alternate to MMC 360 Red admin which is present under Web Admin configuration menu,  
this configuration UI (Default values configuration - Web Admin UI) under Web Admin configuration 
menu can be used to manage Default Value sets and its values.  
This ‘Default values configuration - Web Admin UI’ is not installed as part of the Public 360 or SIF API 
setup, but rather as a separate installation. 
18.4 Example: 
Following example illustrate configuring default value set and pass default value set to SIF 
CreateCaseParameter.  
18.4.1 Configure Default Value Context: 
In the Default value context table ‘MyDefaultValueSet1’ is a SIF default value context. 
Here we can define as many as default value sets we want for.

18.4.3 Configure Default Values: 
The Default value you want to set to a given property of an entity. Here we can have more than one 
default values defined for ‘MyCaseTemplate1’ or for other default value templates. 
 
SIF support single value and multi value properties of objects.  
We can configure multi value properties with two different ways. 
1) Treating multi value property as single value and store its default value as whole in the form of 
json string. 
2) Defining set of values for each sub properties of a multi value property, using dot (.) separator. 
To configure multi value properties you can use any one way out of these 2.

19 Generic SIF Settings 
We have configuration settings which are useful for SIF operation some of them are present in web.config 
file and some of them on 360 web admin setting. In future we have a plan to move all settings in one place 
i.e 360 web admin. 
1. Web config settings 
2. 360 web admin settings 
19.1 Web config settings 
a. If you are using SIF WS service, then you need to add/modify these settings under sif web config 
file. 
Open the IIS, then select SI360 Application Support_360_INST01(in your case the application name may 
be different)  SI.WS.Core  SIF  Explore 
 
Select the “Web.config” file and open it in any editor, so we can modify the setting

file. 
Open the IIS, then select SI360 Application Support_360_INST01(in your case the application name may 
be different)  SI.WS.Core  SIFREST  Explore 
 
Select the “Web.config” file and open it in any editor, so we can modify the setting 
 
 
c. If you are using SIF RPC, then you need to add/modify these settings under SI.WS.Core web 
config file. 
Open the IIS, then select SI360 Application Support_360_INST01(in your case the application name may 
be different)  SI.WS.Core  Explore

19.1.1 SI.Data.SkipValidateResponsible 
We have an app setting SI.Data.SkipValidateResponsible which is default set to false. It validates that the 
integration user is in the same organisation that the responsible org unit and person is on the case or 
document that is being created.  
The error message when it doesn't validate is: “Problem with validation: Contactperson with ID: recno:XX 
does not belong in the same top organisation as OrgUnit with ID: recno:XXX” 
 
How to change setting SI.Data.SkipValidateResponsible: 
Please refer the points a, b and c in chapter 17.1 for how to find location of web.config file 
If the setting is not present then you can also add the below setting as per your requirement for SIF WS, 
REST or RPC else you can update the value either true or false then just save the file. 
 

--- PAGE 330 ---

Public
 
© Tieto

19.1.2 SI.Data.WS.ValidateContactId 
We have an app setting SI.Data.WS.ValidateContactId which is default set to false. It validates the 
ContactId as follows: - 
 
PersonalIdNumber of PrivatePerson: - 
The error message when it validates the PersonalIdNumber: “The field PrivatePersonIdNumber must be a 
number with a minimum length of 11 and a maximum length of 11. It also has to pass mod11 validation” 
 
EnterpriseNumber of Enterprise: - 
The error message when it validates the EnterpriseNumber: “The field EnterpriseNumber must be a 
number with a minimum length of 9 and a maximum length of 9. It also has to pass mod11 validation” 
 
How to change setting SI.Data.WS.ValidateContactId: 
Please refer the points a, b and c in chapter 17.1 for how to find location of web.config file 
If the setting is not present then you can also add the below setting as per your requirement for SIF WS, 
REST or RPC else you can update the value either true or false then just save the file. 
 
 
19.1.3 SI.Data.WS.MaxExceptionLength 
We have an app setting SI.Data.WS.MaxExceptionLength which is default set to 8000. It is used in to set 
the max length for SIF error message and error details. 
 
How to change setting SI.Data.WS.MaxExceptionLength: 
Please refer the points a, b and c in chapter 17.1 for how to find location of web.config file 
If the setting is not present then you can also add the below setting as per your requirement for SIF WS, 
REST or RPC else you can update the value then just save the file.

19.2 360 Web admin settings 
These are the settings which are supported for all SIF WS, REST and RPC. All these settings are added 
through SIF setup if the settings are not found then you need to install the latest SIF package. If you want 
to modify these settings, then refer the following steps: 
 
Open the 360 Administrator then select “Configuration”  “Settings” as shown in following screenshot. 
 
Now select the respective setting then click on Edit after that provide the value for that setting, then just 
click on Save button. Likewise, you can modify the setting value. 
 
One more step here required that whenever we update the setting value then we must need to refersh the 
cache from 360, otherwise the updated setting value will not be reflected.

19.2.3 SIF Show Stack Trace 
Previously in SIF we were returning the stacktrace in the responses from SIF API. That's a security breach. 
We should turn this default off. We should still always get stacktrace in the logs (event log and trace). It 
should be possible to turn on the stacktrace if needed. We have 360-web admin setting to turn on or off 
the stack trace from “SIF Show Stack Trace” setting. 
 
 
How to change setting SIF Show Stack Trace: 
 
Select the setting SIF Show Stack Trace as shown below:  
 
 
 
Just click on Edit button so you can provide value for setting either “Yes” or “No”. “No” to turn off the 
stack trace and “Yes” to turn on the stack trace.

19.2.10 SIF Sync User By Contact Recno Or Contact External Id 
We have 360 web admin setting for synchronising user, "SIF Sync User By Contact Recno Or Contact 
External Id " which is useful to sync the user on the basis of contact recno or contact external id. 
 
Before this implementation suppose if we want to update the login for existing user then it is not possible, 
it will create new user, so we have two entries for that user with old and new user login. So, we introduce 
this new setting. 
 
a. Suppose if we want to update login for the existing user in that case either we can select Contact 
Recno or Contact External Id from this web admin setting like follows: - 
 
b. If you select Contact Recno from the setting, then you should pass Contact Recno in parameter

parameter list

always create the new user. If you want to modify the existing user login then use the setting as 
describe in above points.

19.2.11 SIF Enable ADContextUser 
A new setting has been introduced to make our customers decide whether to be able to use the 
ADContextUser parameter in the SIF API calls (to run in the context of another user than the 
service/integration user).  
This setting is by default disabled (setting value is “No”), meaning you’re not able to use the 
ADContextUser parameter. If you are used to using this parameter, or want to start using it, you can 
enable it again by editing this setting and switching to “Yes” in the dropdown menu. This setting, “SIF 
Enable ADContextUser”, can be found in the list of settings in web admin like with the other SIF settings. 
It looks like this: 
 
 
19.2.12  SIF Log Frequency 
A new setting has been introduced to control the logs generated through SIF API. 
This setting is by default logs only errors. There are two more options available “No Logs” or “Log 
Everything”. If you want more intense debugging, then make use of “Log Everything” option. This setting, 
“SIF Log Frequency”, can be found in the list of settings in web admin like with the other SIF settings. It 
looks like this: 
 
19.2.13 SIF Inherit Responsible From Case (Available from 5.9 version) 
A new setting has been introduced to inherit responsible from case while creating document using 
DocumentService/CreateDocument endpoint. 
This setting is by default set to “Do not inherit”. There are two more options, “Inherit organization unit” 
and “Inherit organization unit and responsible person”. 

--- PAGE 340 ---
 
SIF APIs - Documentation 
 
16 Feb 2026

If set to Inherit organization unit or Inherit organization unit and responsible person, the document will 
inherit responsible from the case. This setting relevant or taken into consideration only for create 
document endpoint (SIF- DocumentService/CreateDocument) and if Responsibles 
(ResponsibleEnterprise and ResponsiblePerson) are not set in the request payload. 
This setting, “SIF Inherit Responsible From Case”, can be found in the list of settings in web admin like 
with the other SIF settings. It looks like this:

19.2.16 SIF LastDate look-up in entitylog (v5.16) 
This setting will be used to improve the performance when searching with LastDate in 
GetCases/GetDocuments/GetFilesWithMetadata. Default set to Yes meaning, search query will do look-
up in EntityLog table along with ModifiedDate to get the result. If set to No, then search query will only 
look-up on ModifiedDate

19.2.17 SIF GetEntitiesExternalIds Config (v5.17) 
This setting should be used to configured external systems for which external ids should be fetched using 
SupportService/GetEntitiesExternaliIds. 
You can have comma separated system name. Default set to empty. 
 
19.2.18 SIF Enable Transactions (v5.17) 
Using this setting you can decide if action should run inside business transaction scope or not. Default it is 
set to true. All the SIF actions will be inside BL transaction scope. If we are outside the BL context, or 
transactions have been disabled with the setting, the action is executed without a transaction scope. 
 
19.2.19 SIF File Inserts in DocumentBatch (v5.17) 
Run files transaction inside document transaction. Default set to No. This setting will be used in 
DocumentService/CreateDocument if request contains files list. 
If set to “Yes”, then all the create document requests will use this setting.

19.2.21 SIF TempEntityStorage Config (v5.18) 
This setting should be used to define which external systems has access to TempEntityStorage through 
SupportService/*TempEntityStorageText endpoints. Please specify a comma (,) separated list of external 
system names. 
Default value is empty, which means that you can’t use the TempEntityStorage endpoints at all. 
 
This setting is available from v5.18 onwards. 
19.2.22 SIF AccessGroup Custom Fields Query (v6.5) 
To get additional 360° fields, which are not currently included in output objects.  
Follow the below steps: 
1. Open 360 admin and go to CONFIGURATION => SETTINGS and edit value for “SIF AccessGroups 
Custom Fields Query” which will include metaquery for getting the custom field, for e.g. 
 
 
<QUERYDESC ENTITY=”AccessGroup” NAMESPACE=”SIRIUS” DATASETFORMAT=”XML”> 
      <RESULTFIELDS> 
            <METAITEM TAG=”CF_testfield”>CF_testfield</METAITEM> 
      </RESULTFIELDS> 
  <CRITERIA> 
        <METAITEM NAME=”Recno” OPERATOR=”IN”> 
          <VALUE>{0}</VALUE> 
        </METAITEM> 
  </CRITERIA>

</QUERYDESC>

<CustomFields>

21 eArchive support 
SIF RPC, which can be used on eArchive to import Cases, Documents and Files. The SIF RPC consists of 
several services but not all are applicable for eArchive. 
This chapter will mainly focus on Case, Document and File service, as all other services can be use like 
today. 
For other services refer the above respective chapter. 
Note: With 360 v5.11, added support for inserting/updating XML fragment through SIF.

16 Feb 2026
Public
 
© Tieto

21.1 Overview model 
This model is a quick overview over the different services and methods that are available in the SIF RPC 
for eArchive. 
 
 
21.2 Update Noark-5 XML Job 
 
This job will run pending updates for Noark5 xml updates. 
You can find this job under “open 360 admin -> Monitoring -> Jobs”

21.3 JSON sample requests 
21.3.1 Archiving case 
For archiving a case, make use of CaseService/CreateCase. The parameters in the data contracts accept 
either code value of the respective code or recno if nothing else is specified. 
For more detail about data contract, refer CreateCase 
 
Correct CaseType, CaseStatus are mandatory to pass in request parameter. 
By default, it will take CaseType: recno:2 “Sak/Case”, so it’s mandatory to pass correct type and status. 
 
{ 
  "parameter": { 
     
    "CaseType": "recno:18", 
    "Title": "Import Case using SIF", 
     
    "Status": "recno:6", 
    "ResponsiblePersonRecno": 1, 
 
     "Contacts": [ 
 
{ 
 
"Role": "recno:17", 
 
"ExternalId": "recno:506" 
         
}, 
 
{ 
 
"Role": "recno:18", 
 
"ExternalId": "recno:1" 
        
} 
 
    ] , 
 "AdditionalFields": [ 
      { 
        "Name": "ImportedCaseNumber", 

--- PAGE 377 ---
 
SIF APIs - Documentation 
 
16 Feb 2026
Public
 
© Tieto 
sif apis - documentation.docx 
Page 377 of 395 
 
        "Value": "12345" 
      } 
    ], 
 
} 
} 
 
You can put this sample JSON parameter in swagger (before executing do necessary changes like 
responsible person recno, imported case number etc.), refer Case 
 
Once case has been imported, you can try to run the “Update Noark-5 xml” job, refer Update Noark-5 XML 
Job 
21.3.2 Get archived case 
Retrieves a list of all archived cases fulfilling the criteria of all the nonblank properties of the supplied 
parameter. At least one search criterion parameter must be supplied. 
You can make use of CaseService/GetCases. 
For contract related details, refer GetCases 
 
Sample JSON GetCases request to get archived case on basis ImportedCaseNumber: 
 
{ 
  "parameter": { 
     
    "AdditionalFields": [ 
      { 
        "Name": "ImportedCaseNumber", 
        "Value": "XXXXX" 
      } 
    ], 
  } 
} 
 
 
21.3.3 Update archived case 
For updating already archived case, use CaseService/UpdateCase. 
Refer UpdateCase, to know more about the parameters for updating a case. 
21.3.4 Archiving document 
For archiving a document, make use of DocumentService/CreateDocument. 
The parameters in the data contracts accept either code value of the respective code or recno if nothing 
else is specified. 

--- PAGE 378 ---
 
SIF APIs - Documentation 
 
16 Feb 2026
Public
 
© Tieto 
sif apis - documentation.docx 
Page 378 of 395 
 
Refer CreateDocument for more detailed explaination of data contracts. 
For archiving document to Earchive, Archive, Status, Category are mandatory. So that it will be imported 
under correct document archive. 
 
Sample JSON request for archiving document: 
 
{ 
  "parameter": { 
    "CaseNumber": "20-11", 
     
    "Archive": "recno:17", 
     
    "Title": "Test document for SIF case", 
     
    "Category": "recno:111", 
    "Status": "recno:6", 
     
    "ResponsiblePersonRecno": 1, 
     
    "Contacts": [ 
      { 
        "Role": "recno:6", 
         
        "ExternalId": "recno:1" 
         
      } 
    ], 
 
"Files": [ 
      { 
        "Title": "SIF Document", 
        "Format": "DOCX", 
         
        "UploadedFileReference": "2ca031b4-51d2-4567-90be-ff502cc9b9a2", 
         
      } 
    ] 
 
     
  } 
} 
 
You can put this sample JSON parameter in swagger refer Document (before executing do necessary 
changes like responsible person recno etc.)

Once document has been imported, you can try to run the “Update Noark-5 xml” job, refer Update Noark-
5 XML Job 
 
21.3.5 Get archived document 
Retrieves a list of all archived documents fulfilling the criteria of all the nonblank properties of the 
supplied parameter. May also be used to get file meta data and the physical file(s) inside the document.  
At least one search criterion parameter must be supplied. 
For contract details refer GetDocuments 
  
Sample JSON GetDocuments request : 
{ 
  "parameter": { 
     
    "AdditionalFields": [ 
      { 
        "Name": "ImportedDocumentNumber", 
        "Value": "XXXXX" 
      } 
    ], 
  } 
} 
 
21.3.6 Update archived document 
For updating already archived case, use DocumentService/UpdateDocument. 
Refer UpdateDocument, to know more about the parameters for updating a document 
 
21.3.7 Upload file reference

    "FileData": [ 
      84,104,105,115,32,105,115,32,97,32,116,101,115,116,32 
    ],

22 Advance endpoint configuration (v5.11 onwards.) 
With version 5.12, we have added support for more advanced endpoint configuration for SIF APIs, to be 
able to provide limited rights, both on methods you invoke or data you retrieve from 360. 
22.1 Filter sets 
We have added two new default value templates/Filter set for CaseService/GetCases and 
DocumentService/GetDocuments. 
You will find these templates under 360 web admin -> Configuration -> Default Values 
 
 
Once you select the required template, you will see some filters set properties like below, 
Note: Creating filter set is exactly same as creating default value set for CaseService/CreateCase and 
DocumentService/CreateDocument 

--- PAGE 385 ---

16 Feb 2026
Public
 
© Tieto

This filter sets you can later use it while configuring the SIF endpoint (as permission set).

22.2 Add endpoint 
We have redesigned the add endpoint config window to provide support for adding filter sets/ default 
values. 
We have added two sections, one for common properties and other is specially for SIF RPC integration 
point. As soon as you select the SIF RPC as integration point, you will see the second section for adding 
filter/default value set. 
 
22.2.1 Common properties 
Refer existing endpoint config document. (Admin guide.) or refer chapter: Endpoint configuration

Service:  
This will contain list of all services offered by SIF endpoint. 
 
Method: 
This will contain list of all methods of service, which is selected in Service control. 
 
Permission set: 
Once you select the Service and Method, you can set filter sets/default values against that configuration. 
 
After saving endpoint with above configurations, you will only be allowed to do CaseService/GetCases 
with this endpoint. Other services or methods won’t be accessible.

22.3 Edit endpoint 
With edit support, you can modify the filter set configuration. You can add more services and methods. 
You can also remove existing configuration. 
To edit, go to endpoint config. Click on edit icon. 
 
 
 
22.4 Delete endpoint 
With delete, along with endpoint, all the filters set configuration will also get deleted. 
22.5 Endpoint information 
Now you can see some more properties of endpoints by hovering over each endpoint.

22.6 Important points to remember 
1. Service, method, and permission set are not mandatory. 
2. Endpoint can have only service configured. 
 
This means that, this endpoint will have access to all the methods under FileService but not 
others. 
3. Service and Method can also be configured without any permission set/default value. 
 
4. Only methods and filter sets are not allowed. 
5. This feature not only support filter set but also default value set while creating case and 
document through SIF API. 
 
 This means when there is CreateCase request, SIF API will pick this configuration and use this 
default value set to create case in P360, you don’t have to pass explicitly into the request. 
 
22.7 Update method filter sets. (v5.14 onwards) 
With version 5.14, we have added support for adding extra layer of security while updating cases and 
documents. Two new filter set templates has been introduced.

SIF.UpdateCaseFilter 
 
SIF.UpdateDocumentFilter 
  
 
 
If any endpoint has been configured with these filters set for UpdateCase or UpdateDocument, API will 
first verify the update request payload with that filter set, if request payload satisfying all the required 
filters, then only API will allow end system to do update on entity.

To configure endpoint with these new filter sets, follow the steps mentioned above. 
 
22.8 Configure result properties to endpoint. (Pilot functionality in SIF in 5.17: Security 
and performance) 
 
To improve performance, security, and data privacy, configure the Get endpoints with only the necessary 
properties in the result set. In this way, you can restrict third parties to having limited data from P360. 
Customers should be able to configure the properties which they want to expose to third parties. With 
this, we make sure that we provide a secured API that follows the privacy by design. Along with security, 
customers will also experience improved performance. 
Improved performance - how? In the new GET implementation, we have got rid of big static queries with 
almost all the joins you can imagine. Now instead we are making queries dynamically depending on the 
filter set and configured properties in the result set. 
This will release in 5.17 with a feature toggle. If any customer wants to try out this cool feature, contact 
SIF team, and we will make it happen! This will be only available for CaseService/GetCases and 
DocumentService/GetDocuments in v5.17
